        /* ── Judul halaman ─────────────────────────────────────────────

           blok, bukan deretan kata lepas. */

        /* ==========================================================
   🚀 HARDENED MODULE ISOLATION (V.2026 ENTERPRISE)
   Aturan mutlak: hanya SATU module yang boleh tampil.
   .module-content = SATU-SATUNYA scroller vertikal per modul.
   ========================================================== */
.module-content {
    display: none !important;
    visibility: hidden;
    pointer-events: none;
    flex-direction: column;
    flex: 1 1 auto;
    min-height: 0;                    /* Syarat mutlak flexbox inner-scroll */
    overflow-y: auto;                 /* Scroll vertikal milik modul, bukan tabel */
    overflow-x: hidden;
    position: relative;
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
    /* 🔧 contain: layout style DIHAPUS — properti ini menciptakan
       formatting context & stacking context baru yang pada sebagian
       engine mengganggu perilaku position:sticky milik anak-anaknya. */
}
.module-content.module-active {
    display: flex !important;
    visibility: visible;
    pointer-events: auto;
    animation: moduleFadeIn 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1) both;
}
/* 🔧 [FIX] `animation: fadeIn` dirujuk sejak lama tetapi @keyframes fadeIn
   TIDAK PERNAH didefinisikan di berkas ini → nama animasi tak dikenal =
   no-op senyap, sehingga modul "menjeplak" muncul tanpa transisi. */
@keyframes moduleFadeIn {
    from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); }
    to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
}

/* 🛡️ ANTI-SQUEEZE GUARD (AKAR BUG "HALAMAN TIDAK MEMANJANG"):
   Dalam flex column dengan tinggi pasti, anak dengan flex-shrink:1
   DIPAKSA menyusut ke min-height-nya → konten terjepit → scroll
   pindah ke perangkap internal. Aturan ini melarang SEMUA shell
   modul menyusut: tinggi shell = tinggi konten → modul memanjang
   ke bawah secara natural dan .module-content yang men-scroll. */
#module-container > .module-content > * {
    flex-shrink: 0;
}
/* ==========================================================================
   🔐 ENTERPRISE LOGIN GATEWAY — SPLIT PANEL EDITION (V.2026)
   Kontrak layer:
     #login-gateway  → layer absolut tertinggi (2147483647)
     body.gateway-locked → dashboard disembunyikan TOTAL (bukan blur).
     ⚠️ `filter:blur()` pada #view-dashboard DIHAPUS: properti filter
        menciptakan containing-block baru sehingga #sidebar (position:fixed)
        ikut ter-reposisi, dan biaya repaint-nya mahal pada DOM sebesar ini.
   ========================================================================== */
#login-gateway {
    position: fixed; inset: 0;
    z-index: 2147483647;
    isolation: isolate;
    display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
    padding: 1.75rem 1.25rem;
    background:
        radial-gradient(1200px 700px at 20% 12%, #17253f 0%, transparent 60%),
        radial-gradient(900px 600px at 85% 88%, #12203a 0%, transparent 62%),
        linear-gradient(160deg, #0b1428 0%, #060d1d 55%, #020617 100%);
    overflow-y: auto;
    transition: opacity .55s ease, visibility .55s ease;
}

/* 🔒 Kunci layer saat gateway aktif */
body.gateway-locked { overflow: hidden !important; }
body.gateway-locked #global-loader   { z-index: 2147483646; }
body.gateway-locked .swal2-container { z-index: 2147483645 !important; }

/* ==========================================================================
   🎬 KOREOGRAFI MASUK — mengapa dulu terasa "nge-crash":
   Versi lama melepas `gateway-locked` pada detik yang SAMA dengan mulainya
   fade gateway. Akibatnya seluruh dashboard (sidebar, navbar, tabel modul)
   muncul mendadak pada opacity penuh di balik gateway yang masih separuh
   transparan — pengguna melihat kilatan tata letak setengah jadi, lalu
   #main-wrapper dianimasikan LAGI oleh `app-ready` → kedipan kedua.

   Sekarang: dashboard tetap tersembunyi selama `app-booting`, dibuka HANYA
   setelah modul terpasang dan browser sempat melukis satu bingkai.
   ========================================================================== */
#view-dashboard {
    opacity: 1; visibility: visible;
    transition: opacity .45s ease, visibility 0s linear 0s;
}
body.gateway-locked #view-dashboard,
body.app-booting    #view-dashboard {
    opacity: 0; visibility: hidden; pointer-events: none !important;
    transition: opacity .25s ease, visibility 0s linear .25s;
}
/* ⚠️ SENGAJA hanya `opacity` — TIDAK ADA `transform` di sini.
   #view-dashboard membungkus #sidebar yang ber-position:fixed; transform
   sekecil apa pun pada leluhur mengubahnya menjadi containing block baru
   sehingga sidebar (dan sticky header di dalamnya) ikut ter-reposisi
   selama animasi. Kesan "naik" tetap didapat dari moduleFadeIn milik
   .module-active, yang aman karena berada di dalam alur normal. */

/* Kartu login ikut mundur secara sengaja, bukan terpotong mendadak. */
#login-gateway.gateway-hidden .lg-shell {
    animation: none !important;
    opacity: 0; transform: translateY(-18px) scale(.965);
    transition: opacity .38s ease, transform .5s cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1);
}

/* ==========================================================================
   🛡️ PERISAI AUTOFILL
   Setelah logout, browser mengisi ulang NAMA AKUN / KATA SANDI dan memaksa
   latar putih-kebiruan bawaannya. Teks kita berwarna putih → kolom tampak
   "terlalu cerah" dan isinya nyaris tak terbaca.
   Latar autofill TIDAK bisa ditimpa `background`; satu-satunya cara yang
   diterima seluruh mesin WebKit/Blink adalah menutupinya dengan inset
   box-shadow setebal kolom, plus mengunci warna teks via -webkit-text-fill-color.
   ========================================================================== */
.lg-input-wrap input:-webkit-autofill,
.lg-input-wrap input:-webkit-autofill:hover,
.lg-input-wrap input:-webkit-autofill:focus,
.lg-input-wrap input:-webkit-autofill:active,
.lg-input-wrap input:autofill {
    -webkit-text-fill-color: #ffffff !important;
    caret-color: var(--accent-gold) !important;
    -webkit-box-shadow: 0 0 0 60px #050b1a inset !important;
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 60px #050b1a inset !important;
    background-color: transparent !important;
    background-image: none !important;
    border-radius: 0 !important;
    font-weight: 600 !important;
    /* Menahan animasi latar bawaan browser agar tidak "berkelip" saat fokus. */
    transition: background-color 600000s 0s, color 600000s 0s !important;
}
.lg-input-wrap:focus-within input:-webkit-autofill,
.lg-input-wrap:focus-within input:autofill {
    -webkit-box-shadow: 0 0 0 60px #02060f inset !important;
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 60px #02060f inset !important;
}
/* Firefox */
.lg-input-wrap input:-moz-autofill,
.lg-input-wrap input:-moz-autofill-preview {
    filter: none;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 60px #050b1a inset;
    -moz-text-fill-color: #ffffff;
}

/* ==========================================
           🔝 STACKING HIERARCHY GUARD (ANTI-OVERLAP)
           Navbar & dropdown profil HARUS selalu di atas
           konten modul (sticky controls, tabel, dll).
           ========================================== */
        .top-navbar .dropdown-menu {
            z-index: 1080 !important;
        }
        #module-container {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 1;
        }

        /* ==========================================================
   🧲 SCROLL CHAIN GUARD (GLOBAL — SEMUA MODUL)
   Bug: wrapper tabel ber-overflow-x:auto dikomputasi browser
   menjadi overflow-y:auto → menjadi scroll container vertikal
   diam-diam → roda mouse "dimakan" saat kursor di atas tabel
   → halaman modul terasa MATI.
   Hukum baru: wrapper tabel HANYA boleh scroll horizontal.
   Tinggi wajib mengikuti konten, sehingga wheel event selalu
   diteruskan (chaining) ke .module-content.
   ========================================================== */
#module-container .module-content .table-responsive,
#module-container .module-content .custom-scroll-area,
#module-container .module-content [class*="scroll-area"] {
    max-height: none !important;
    height: auto !important;
    flex-shrink: 0 !important;
    overscroll-behavior: auto !important; /* wajib auto agar chaining hidup */
}
    </style>

                            <li><a class="dropdown-item py-2 fw-medium d-flex align-items-center gap-2" href="#" onclick="return switchModule(event, 'profile')"><i class="bi bi-person-vcard text-primary"></i> Profile Karyawan</a></li>
                            <li id="nav-user-manager" hidden><a class="dropdown-item py-2 fw-medium d-flex align-items-center gap-2" href="#" data-module="user_manager" onclick="return switchModule(event, 'user_manager')"><i class="bi bi-shield-lock-fill text-warning"></i> Master Kredensial</a></li>
                            <li><hr class="dropdown-divider my-2"></li>

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>

    <script>
        // =========================================================================
        // GLOBAL APP STATE & JS CORE EVENT MANAGER (LAZY SYNC PROTOCOL)
        // =========================================================================
        const APP_STATE = {
            activeModule: null,
            bootModule: 'stock_tab',   // 🥇 Modul boot mutlak (backend prioritas)
            bootTab:    'MASUK',
            isSwitching: false,
            isBooted:    false,        // 🔧 [FIX B3] guard boot ganda
            clockInterval: null,
            initializedModules: {},
            /**
             * 🔧 [FIX B9-R] Peta dirty TIDAK lagi ditulis tangan.
             * Daftar manual lama sempat menyimpan key yatim ('kode_stock' —
             * tidak punya container #mod-*) sekaligus melewatkan modul baru,
             * sehingga sebagian modul tak pernah ikut lazy-sync.
             * Kini diturunkan otomatis dari MODULE_REGISTRY (lihat BAB LAZY-SYNC)
             * → mustahil melenceng dari kenyataan.
             */
            dirtyModules: {}
        };
        let html5QrCode = null;
        let primeCameraStream = null;

        // =========================================================================
        // 🛡️ BAB B: BACKEND PRIORITY GATE (JS PRIME BOOT ARBITER)
        // ⚠️ TIDAK ADA TAG <script> DI SINI — ini bagian dari script induk.
        // =========================================================================
        const JSBackend = (function () {
            let _gateOpen    = false;
            let _draining    = false;              // 🔧 [F4] cegah drain ganda
            const _primeOwner = 'stock_tab';       // 🔧 const (tak pernah reassign)
            const _queue     = [];
            let _drainTimer  = null;

            function _dispatch(job) {
                try {
                    let runner = google.script.run;
                    if (job.onSuccess) runner = runner.withSuccessHandler(job.onSuccess);
                    if (job.onFailure) runner = runner.withFailureHandler(job.onFailure);
                    if (typeof runner[job.fn] !== 'function') {
                        console.error(`[JS BACKEND] Endpoint tidak ditemukan: ${job.fn}`);
                        if (job.onFailure) job.onFailure(new Error('ENDPOINT_NOT_FOUND: ' + job.fn));
                        return;
                    }
                    runner[job.fn].apply(runner, job.args);
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error('[JS BACKEND] Dispatch error:', e);
                    if (job.onFailure) job.onFailure(e);
                }
            }

            function _drain() {
                if (_draining) return;             // 🔧 [F4] guard
                _draining = true;
                const step = () => {
                    if (!_queue.length) { _draining = false; return; }
                    _dispatch(_queue.shift());
                    _drainTimer = setTimeout(step, 120);   // stagger anti-LockService burst
                };
                step();
            }

            const api = {
                run(fn, args = [], opt = {}) {
                    if (typeof google === 'undefined' || !google.script || !google.script.run) {
                        console.warn('[JS BACKEND] Dev Mode: google.script.run tidak tersedia.');
                        if (opt.onFailure) opt.onFailure(new Error('NO_BACKEND'));
                        return;
                    }
                    const job = {
                        fn, args,
                        onSuccess: opt.onSuccess || null,
                        onFailure: opt.onFailure || null,
                        owner    : opt.owner || 'unknown'
                    };
                    if (_gateOpen || opt.priority === true || job.owner === _primeOwner) {
                        _dispatch(job);
                        return;
                    }
                    console.log(`[JS BACKEND] Antre (gate closed): ${fn} <- ${job.owner}`);
                    _queue.push(job);
                },

                openGate(reason = 'stock_tab-ready') {
                    if (_gateOpen) return;
                    _gateOpen = true;
                    document.body.classList.remove('backend-booting');
                    console.log(`[JS BACKEND] 🔓 Gerbang DIBUKA (${reason}). Antrean: ${_queue.length}`);
                    _drain();
                },

                armFailsafe(ms = 8000) {
                    setTimeout(function () {
                        if (!_gateOpen) {
                            console.warn('[JS BACKEND] ⏱️ Failsafe — gerbang dipaksa buka.');
                            api.openGate('failsafe-timeout');   // 🔧 [F5] closure, bukan `this`
                        }
                    }, ms);
                },

                isOpen()    { return _gateOpen; },
                queueSize() { return _queue.length; }
            };
            return api;
        })();
        window.JSBackend = JSBackend;

        // =========================================================================
        // 🔐 BAB B2: AUTH GATEWAY (SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH OTORISASI)
        // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Kontrak arsitektur (V.2026):
        //   1. TIDAK ADA modul / panggilan backend yang boot sebelum login sukses.
        //      (sebelumnya StockTab menembus gerbang sebagai _primeOwner → data
        //       tertarik oleh pengunjung yang belum terotorisasi).
        //   2. Login sukses → hydrate JSEngine.session → boot aplikasi → buka
        //      gerbang JSBackend → tembak 'onStockTabBooted'.
        //   3. Logout / idle-timeout → teardown penuh, kembali ke gateway bersih.
        // =========================================================================
        const SESSION = { user: null, authenticated: false };

        // 🌐 GLOBAL SESSION BRIDGE — dibaca modul Profile, StockForm, dll.
        // (dipelihara otomatis oleh JSSession.mirror() di bawah)
        window.APP_SESSION = { userId: null, user: null, authenticated: false };

        /* =========================================================================
         * 🪪 JSSession — BUS IDENTITAS TUNGGAL (Index ⇄ semua modul)
         * -------------------------------------------------------------------------
         * Masalah yang diselesaikan:
         *   Sebelumnya identitas login hanya "dilempar" ke window.APP_SESSION lalu
         *   modul lain menebak-nebak bentuknya (namaAkun / NAMA AKUN / NAMA_AKUN),
         *   dan Profile terpaksa POLLING setiap 250 ms untuk menunggu sesi muncul.
         *   Tidak ada pula kanal untuk memberi tahu modul ketika profil BERUBAH,
         *   sehingga navbar tetap menampilkan nama lama setelah Profile disunting.
         *
         * Kontrak:
         *   JSSession.set(user)      → normalisasi + siarkan 'onSessionReady'
         *   JSSession.patch(fields)  → merge + siarkan 'onSessionUpdated'
         *   JSSession.clear()        → hapus + siarkan 'onSessionCleared'
         *   JSSession.getUser()      → objek kanonik (selalu bentuk yang sama)
         *   JSSession.subscribe(fn)  → dipanggil segera bila sesi sudah ada
         * ========================================================================= */
        const JSSession = (function () {
            'use strict';

            let _user = null;
            const _subs = new Set();

            const S = (v) => (v === null || v === undefined) ? '' : String(v).trim();
            const U = (v) => S(v).toUpperCase();

            /** Menerima bentuk apa pun (payload login ATAU baris DB) → bentuk kanonik. */
            function normalize(raw) {
                if (!raw) return null;
                const g = (...keys) => {
                    for (const k of keys) {
                        const v = raw[k];
                        if (v !== null && v !== undefined && v !== '' && v !== '***PROTECTED***') return v;
                    }
                    return '';
                };
                return {
                    id            : U(g('id', 'idUser', 'ID USER', 'ID_USER', 'JS ID')),
                    namaAkun      : U(g('namaAkun', 'NAMA AKUN', 'NAMA_AKUN', 'akun')),
                    nama          : U(g('nama', 'namaLengkap', 'NAMA LENGKAP', 'NAMA_LENGKAP')),
                    namaPanggilan : U(g('namaPanggilan', 'NAMA', 'NAMA PANGGILAN')),
                    role          : U(g('role', 'ROLE')) || 'PEGAWAI',
                    level         : S(g('level', 'LEVEL')) || '1',
                    status        : U(g('status', 'STATUS')) || 'AKTIF',
                    foto          : S(g('foto', 'FOTO')),
                    email         : S(g('email', 'EMAIL')),
                    noTelp        : S(g('noTelp', 'NO TELP', 'NO_TELP')),
                    alamat        : U(g('alamat', 'ALAMAT')),
                    tanggalLahir  : S(g('tanggalLahir', 'TANGGAL LAHIR', 'TANGGAL_LAHIR', 'TANGGAL')),
                    rowIndex      : g('rowIndex', '_rowIndex', '_ROWINDEX') || null,
                    loginAt       : raw.loginAt || null
                };
            }

            /** Menjaga window.APP_SESSION (kontrak lama SharedEngine) tetap sinkron. */
            function mirror() {
                window.APP_SESSION = _user
                    ? { userId: _user.id || _user.namaAkun, user: _user, authenticated: true }
                    : { userId: null, user: null, authenticated: false };
            }

            function emit(evt) {
                const snap = _user ? Object.assign({}, _user) : null;
                _subs.forEach(fn => { try { fn(snap, evt); } catch (e) { console.warn('[SESSION] subscriber:', e); } });
                if (typeof window.tembakEventJS === 'function') window.tembakEventJS(evt, snap);
            }

            return {
                normalize,

                set(raw) {
                    _user = normalize(raw);
                    if (_user) _user.loginAt = _user.loginAt || new Date().toISOString();
                    mirror();
                    emit('onSessionReady');
                    return _user;
                },

                /** Merge parsial — dipakai Profile setelah biodata/foto disimpan. */
                patch(fields) {
                    if (!_user || !fields) return null;
                    const inc = normalize(Object.assign({}, _user, fields));
                    // Field kosong pada patch tidak boleh menghapus nilai lama.
                    Object.keys(inc).forEach(k => { if (inc[k] !== '' && inc[k] !== null) _user[k] = inc[k]; });
                    mirror();
                    emit('onSessionUpdated');
                    return Object.assign({}, _user);
                },

                clear() {
                    _user = null;
                    mirror();
                    emit('onSessionCleared');
                },

                getUser()        { return _user ? Object.assign({}, _user) : null; },
                isAuthenticated(){ return _user !== null; },

                /** Langsung dipanggil bila sesi sudah siap → modul tak perlu polling. */
                subscribe(fn) {
                    if (typeof fn !== 'function') return () => {};
                    _subs.add(fn);
                    if (_user) { try { fn(Object.assign({}, _user), 'onSessionReady'); } catch (e) {} }
                    return () => _subs.delete(fn);
                }
            };
        })();
        window.JSSession = JSSession;

        /* =========================================================================
         * 🖼️ Resolver avatar bersama — URL / base64 / path Drive / ID Drive.
         * Dipakai chip navbar & modul Profile agar logikanya tidak dua versi.
         * ========================================================================= */
        const _avatarCache  = Object.create(null);   // path → sumber gambar matang
        const _avatarInFlight = Object.create(null); // path → daftar callback menunggu

        /**
         * 🖼️ Resolver gambar profil bersama (navbar · Profile · UserManager).
         *
         * ⚠️ [FIX] Versi lama memakai `apiGetUserImage`, yang mengembalikan URL
         * bertipe `https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=...`. Di dalam iframe
         * sandbox Google Apps Script, URL itu SERING ditolak Drive (redirect ke
         * halaman login) sehingga <img> gagal memuat dan avatar selalu jatuh ke
         * huruf inisial — persis gejala "kolom FOTO tidak menampilkan gambar".
         *
         * Sekarang jalur utamanya `apiGetImageHDBase64`, yang mengunduh berkas di
         * SISI SERVER lalu mengirim data URI base64. Data URI tidak pernah
         * terpengaruh cookie, redirect, maupun kebijakan lintas-asal — gambar
         * dijamin tampil. `apiGetUserImage` dipertahankan sebagai cadangan.
         */
        window.resolveAvatarSrc = function (raw, onReady) {
            const cb = (typeof onReady === 'function') ? onReady : function () {};
            const s = String(raw || '').trim();
            if (!s || s === '-') { cb(''); return; }
            if (s.startsWith('data:image')) { cb(s); return; }
            if (_avatarCache[s]) { cb(_avatarCache[s]); return; }

            // URL http di luar wilayah Google → aman dipakai langsung.
            const wilayahDrive = /drive\.google\.com|googleusercontent\.com|lh\d\.google/.test(s);
            if (s.startsWith('http') && !wilayahDrive) { cb(s); return; }

            // 🚦 Dedup permintaan berjalan: chip navbar, Profile, dan tabel
            //    UserManager meminta foto yang SAMA pada milidetik berdekatan.
            //    Tanpa antrean ini Drive dipanggil berkali-kali untuk satu berkas.
            if (_avatarInFlight[s]) { _avatarInFlight[s].push(cb); return; }
            _avatarInFlight[s] = [cb];

            const selesai = (url) => {
                if (url) _avatarCache[s] = url;
                const waiting = _avatarInFlight[s] || [];
                delete _avatarInFlight[s];
                waiting.forEach(fn => { try { fn(url); } catch (e) { console.warn('[AVATAR]', e); } });
            };

            if (!window.JSBackend || typeof window.JSBackend.run !== 'function') { selesai(''); return; }

            const opsi = { owner: 'identity', priority: true };

            // Cadangan: resolver lama (mengembalikan URL Drive). Hanya dipakai
            // bila jalur base64 gagal — lebih baik gambar berpeluang tampil
            // daripada tidak sama sekali.
            const cadangan = () => window.JSBackend.run('apiGetUserImage', [s], Object.assign({}, opsi, {
                onSuccess: (res) => selesai((res && res.status && (res.url || res.dataUri)) || ''),
                onFailure: () => selesai('')
            }));

            window.JSBackend.run('apiGetImageHDBase64', [s], Object.assign({}, opsi, {
                onSuccess: (res) => {
                    const uri = res && res.status && (res.dataUri || res.url);
                    if (uri) selesai(uri); else cadangan();
                },
                onFailure: cadangan
            }));
        };

        const AuthGateway = (function () {
            'use strict';

            const CFG = {
                MAX_ATTEMPTS   : 5,
                LOCKOUT_MS     : 30000,          // cooldown klien setelah gagal beruntun
                IDLE_MS        : 30 * 60 * 1000, // auto-logout idle
                IDLE_WARN_MS   : 60 * 1000,      // peringatan sebelum idle-logout
                REMEMBER_KEY   : 'JS_PRIME_REMEMBER_AKUN',
                LOCKOUT_KEY    : 'JS_PRIME_LOCKOUT_UNTIL',
                HIDE_DELAY_MS  : 620
            };

            const IDLE_EVENTS = ['mousemove', 'mousedown', 'keydown', 'wheel', 'touchstart', 'scroll'];

            let _failCount    = 0;
            let _lockUntil    = 0;
            let _lockTicker   = null;
            let _hideTimer    = null;
            let _idleTimer    = null;
            let _idleWarnTimer= null;
            let _idleArmed    = false;
            let _submitting   = false;
            let _bound        = false;   // guard: bind() idempotent (anti listener ganda)

            // ── util DOM aman ────────────────────────────────────────────────
            const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

            function _safeStore(action, key, value) {
                try {
                    if (action === 'get')    return localStorage.getItem(key);
                    if (action === 'set')    { localStorage.setItem(key, value); return true; }
                    if (action === 'remove') { localStorage.removeItem(key); return true; }
                } catch (e) { /* private mode / quota */ }
                return null;
            }

            // ── UMPAN BALIK UI ───────────────────────────────────────────────
            function showError(msg) {
                const el = $('loginError');
                if (!el) return;
                el.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill"></i><span></span>';
                el.querySelector('span').textContent = msg;   // ⛔ anti-injeksi
                el.classList.add('show');
                ['lgWrapAkun', 'lgWrapSandi'].forEach(id => { const w = $(id); if (w) w.classList.add('is-invalid'); });
                const shell = $('lgShell');
                if (shell) { shell.classList.remove('lg-reject'); void shell.offsetWidth; shell.classList.add('lg-reject'); }
            }

            function clearError() {
                const el = $('loginError');
                if (el) { el.classList.remove('show'); el.innerHTML = ''; }
                ['lgWrapAkun', 'lgWrapSandi'].forEach(id => { const w = $(id); if (w) w.classList.remove('is-invalid'); });
            }

            function setLoading(active) {
                const btn = $('loginSubmitBtn');
                if (!btn) return;
                btn.disabled = !!active || isLocked();
                btn.classList.toggle('is-loading', !!active);
            }

            // ── LOCKOUT KLIEN (anti brute-force ringan) ──────────────────────
            function isLocked() { return Date.now() < _lockUntil; }

            function _renderLockout() {
                const box = $('loginLockout');
                const btn = $('loginSubmitBtn');
                if (!box) return;

                const remain = Math.ceil((_lockUntil - Date.now()) / 1000);
                if (remain <= 0) {
                    box.classList.remove('show'); box.innerHTML = '';
                    if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
                    if (_lockTicker) { clearInterval(_lockTicker); _lockTicker = null; }
                    _failCount = 0;
                    _safeStore('remove', CFG.LOCKOUT_KEY);
                    return;
                }
                box.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-hourglass-split"></i><span></span>';
                box.querySelector('span').textContent =
                    `Terlalu banyak percobaan gagal. Terminal terkunci ${remain} detik.`;
                box.classList.add('show');
                if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
            }

            function engageLockout(ms) {
                _lockUntil = Date.now() + ms;
                _safeStore('set', CFG.LOCKOUT_KEY, String(_lockUntil));
                clearError();
                _renderLockout();
                if (_lockTicker) clearInterval(_lockTicker);
                _lockTicker = setInterval(_renderLockout, 500);
            }

            function restoreLockout() {
                const raw = parseInt(_safeStore('get', CFG.LOCKOUT_KEY) || '0', 10);
                if (raw && raw > Date.now()) {
                    _lockUntil = raw;
                    _failCount = CFG.MAX_ATTEMPTS;
                    _renderLockout();
                    if (_lockTicker) clearInterval(_lockTicker);
                    _lockTicker = setInterval(_renderLockout, 500);
                } else if (raw) {
                    _safeStore('remove', CFG.LOCKOUT_KEY);
                }
            }

            // ── CAPS LOCK & TOGGLE PASSWORD ──────────────────────────────────
            function handleCaps(e) {
                const hint = $('loginCapsHint');
                if (!hint || typeof e.getModifierState !== 'function') return;
                hint.classList.toggle('show', e.getModifierState('CapsLock'));
            }

            function togglePass() {
                const inp  = $('loginKataSandi');
                const icon = $('loginEyeIcon');
                const btn  = $('loginTogglePass');
                if (!inp || !icon) return;
                const reveal = inp.type === 'password';
                inp.type  = reveal ? 'text' : 'password';
                icon.className = reveal ? 'bi bi-eye-slash-fill' : 'bi bi-eye-fill';
                if (btn) {
                    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(reveal));
                    btn.setAttribute('aria-label', reveal ? 'Sembunyikan kata sandi' : 'Tampilkan kata sandi');
                }
                inp.focus();
            }

            // ── LOGIN ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            function login() {
                if (_submitting) return;                       // anti double-submit (Enter + klik)
                if (isLocked())  { _renderLockout(); return; }

                const akunEl  = $('loginNamaAkun');
                const sandiEl = $('loginKataSandi');
                const namaAkun  = akunEl  ? akunEl.value.trim()  : '';
                const kataSandi = sandiEl ? sandiEl.value.trim() : '';

                clearError();

                if (!namaAkun || !kataSandi) {
                    showError('Nama Akun dan Kata Sandi wajib diisi.');
                    (namaAkun ? sandiEl : akunEl)?.focus();
                    return;
                }

                _submitting = true;
                setLoading(true);

                // 🥇 priority:true → login SELALU menembus gerbang backend.
                window.JSBackend.run('apiVerifikasiLoginStock', [namaAkun, kataSandi], {
                    owner: 'auth_gateway',
                    priority: true,
                    onSuccess: function (res) {
                        _submitting = false;
                        setLoading(false);

                        if (res && res.status === true && res.user) {
                            _failCount = 0;
                            _safeStore('remove', CFG.LOCKOUT_KEY);
                            unlock(res.user);
                            return;
                        }

                        _failCount++;
                        const sisa = CFG.MAX_ATTEMPTS - _failCount;
                        if (sisa <= 0) {
                            engageLockout(CFG.LOCKOUT_MS);
                        } else {
                            let msg = (res && res.message) ? String(res.message) : 'Verifikasi gagal.';
                            if (sisa <= 2) msg += ` Sisa ${sisa} percobaan.`;
                            showError(msg);
                        }
                        if (sandiEl) { sandiEl.value = ''; sandiEl.focus(); }
                    },
                    onFailure: function (err) {
                        _submitting = false;
                        setLoading(false);
                        console.error('[AUTH] Kegagalan transport:', err);
                        showError('Koneksi ke server terputus. Periksa jaringan lalu coba lagi.');
                    }
                });
            }

            // ── BUKA GERBANG (PASCA-OTORISASI) ───────────────────────────────
            function unlock(user) {
                SESSION.user = user;
                SESSION.authenticated = true;

                // 🪪 Satu panggilan → APP_SESSION ter-mirror, event 'onSessionReady'
                //    disiarkan, dan SEMUA modul (Profile, dst.) terisi otomatis.
                const canon = JSSession.set(user);

                // 🔑 KONTRAK WAJIB SharedEngine: tanpa hydrate(), kolom NAMA PEGAWAI
                //    tertulis kosong pada seluruh mutasi stok (audit trail anonim).
                try {
                    if (window.JSEngine && window.JSEngine.session && typeof window.JSEngine.session.hydrate === 'function') {
                        window.JSEngine.session.hydrate(canon || user);
                    }
                } catch (e) { console.warn('[AUTH] Hydrate session gagal:', e); }

                applyRoleVisibility(canon || user);
                persistRemember();

                /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
                   🎬 KOREOGRAFI MASUK (4 LANGKAH)
                   Urutannya disengaja — inilah yang dulu membuat perpindahan
                   terasa "nge-crash":

                   ① Tirai dipasang SEKETIKA di balik gateway. Latarnya identik
                      dengan gateway, jadi belum ada yang berubah di mata.
                   ② `app-booting` menahan dashboard tetap tersembunyi meski
                      `gateway-locked` sudah dilepas (dulu keduanya dilepas
                      bersamaan → tata letak setengah jadi sempat terlihat).
                   ③ Gateway memudar; yang tersingkap hanyalah tirai.
                   ④ Modul selesai dipasang → revealApp() membuka dashboard
                      dengan satu transisi tunggal.
                   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

                // ① Tirai turun lebih dulu — tanpa fade-in.
                if (typeof showLoader === 'function') showLoader('Menyiapkan terminal…', true);

                // ② Dashboard tetap tertutup selama proses boot.
                document.body.classList.add('app-booting');
                document.body.classList.remove('gateway-locked');

                // ③ Gateway mundur di atas tirai.
                const gateway = $('login-gateway');
                if (gateway) {
                    gateway.classList.add('gateway-hidden');
                    if (_hideTimer) clearTimeout(_hideTimer);
                    _hideTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                        if (gateway.classList.contains('gateway-hidden')) gateway.style.display = 'none';
                    }, CFG.HIDE_DELAY_MS);
                }

                // ④ Boot aplikasi; dashboard baru dibuka oleh revealApp().
                if (typeof window.__bootApplication === 'function') window.__bootApplication();

                armIdleWatch();
                // Sapaan menunggu tirai naik agar tidak menumpuk di atas transisi.
                const sapa = user.namaPanggilan || user.nama || user.namaAkun;
                setTimeout(() => {
                    if (typeof showToast === 'function' && SESSION.authenticated) {
                        showToast(`Selamat datang kembali, ${sapa}.`, 'success');
                    }
                }, 1150);
            }

            /**
             * 🔐 Menu berperan tinggi — Stock Center & Master Kredensial.
             * Keduanya hanya untuk MASTER / OWNER. Sebelumnya Master Kredensial
             * terlihat oleh SEMUA peran, sehingga pegawai biasa dapat membuka
             * layar pembuatan akun dan mengangkat dirinya sendiri jadi MASTER.
             * Penyembunyian menu ini adalah lapis pertama; modul UserManager
             * menegakkan lapis kedua lewat layar "akses ditolak".
             */
            const ROLE_TINGGI = ['MASTER', 'OWNER', 'MASTER/OWNER'];

            function applyRoleVisibility(user) {
                const role  = String((user && user.role) || '').toUpperCase().trim();
                const boleh = ROLE_TINGGI.includes(role);

                const nav = $('nav-stock-center');
                if (nav) nav.style.setProperty('display', boleh ? 'flex' : 'none', 'important');

                const um = $('nav-user-manager');
                if (um) um.hidden = !boleh;
            }

            /**
             * 🪪 Render chip identitas navbar dari objek sesi kanonik.
             * Tidak dipanggil manual — terhubung ke JSSession.subscribe() sehingga
             * otomatis ikut berubah saat login, saat Profile disunting, dan saat logout.
             */
            function renderIdentity(user) {
                const set = (id, text) => { const el = $(id); if (el) el.textContent = text; };
                const initial = (str) => {
                    const m = String(str || '').match(/[A-Za-z0-9]/);
                    return m ? m[0].toUpperCase() : '?';
                };

                if (!user) {
                    set('nbChipName', 'Belum masuk');
                    set('nbChipRole', '—');
                    set('nbMenuName', 'Belum masuk');
                    set('nbMenuHandle', '@—');
                    set('nbMenuRole', '—');
                    set('nbMenuId', '—');
                    set('nbAvatarInitial', '?');
                    set('nbMenuAvatarInitial', '?');
                    ['nbAvatarImg', 'nbMenuAvatarImg'].forEach(id => {
                        const img = $(id); if (img) { img.hidden = true; img.removeAttribute('src'); }
                    });
                    ['nbAvatarInitial', 'nbMenuAvatarInitial'].forEach(id => {
                        const el = $(id); if (el) el.hidden = false;
                    });
                    const dot = $('nbAvatarDot'); if (dot) dot.classList.add('is-offline');
                    return;
                }

                const tampil = user.namaPanggilan || user.nama || user.namaAkun || 'Pengguna';
                const ini    = initial(user.nama || user.namaAkun);

                set('nbChipName',  tampil);
                set('nbChipRole',  user.role || 'PEGAWAI');
                set('nbMenuName',  user.nama || user.namaAkun || 'Pengguna Terminal');
                set('nbMenuHandle', '@' + (user.namaAkun || '—'));
                set('nbMenuRole',  user.role || 'PEGAWAI');
                set('nbMenuId',    user.id || '—');
                set('nbAvatarInitial', ini);
                set('nbMenuAvatarInitial', ini);

                const dot = $('nbAvatarDot'); if (dot) dot.classList.remove('is-offline');

                // Foto asli diambil belakangan; inisial tampil lebih dulu (paint 0 ms).
                window.resolveAvatarSrc(user.foto, (url) => {
                    [['nbAvatarImg', 'nbAvatarInitial'], ['nbMenuAvatarImg', 'nbMenuAvatarInitial']]
                        .forEach(([imgId, iniId]) => {
                            const img = $(imgId), txt = $(iniId);
                            if (!img || !txt) return;
                            if (!url) { img.hidden = true; txt.hidden = false; return; }
                            img.onload  = () => { img.hidden = false; txt.hidden = true;  };
                            img.onerror = () => { img.hidden = true;  txt.hidden = false; };
                            img.src = url;
                        });
                });
            }

            // 🔌 Chip navbar mengikuti sesi secara otomatis (login / patch / logout).
            JSSession.subscribe(renderIdentity);

            function persistRemember() {
                const cb   = $('loginRemember');
                const akun = $('loginNamaAkun');
                if (!cb || !akun) return;
                if (cb.checked && akun.value.trim()) _safeStore('set', CFG.REMEMBER_KEY, akun.value.trim());
                else _safeStore('remove', CFG.REMEMBER_KEY);
            }

            function restoreRemember() {
                const saved = _safeStore('get', CFG.REMEMBER_KEY);
                const akun  = $('loginNamaAkun');
                const cb    = $('loginRemember');
                if (saved && akun) { akun.value = saved; if (cb) cb.checked = true; }
                return !!saved;
            }

            // ── IDLE WATCHDOG ────────────────────────────────────────────────
            function _clearIdleTimers() {
                if (_idleTimer)     { clearTimeout(_idleTimer);     _idleTimer = null; }
                if (_idleWarnTimer) { clearTimeout(_idleWarnTimer); _idleWarnTimer = null; }
            }

            function resetIdle() {
                if (!SESSION.authenticated) return;
                _clearIdleTimers();
                _idleWarnTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                    if (!SESSION.authenticated) return;
                    try {
                        if (typeof Swal !== 'undefined') {
                            Swal.fire({
                                title: 'Sesi akan berakhir',
                                html: '<p class="text-muted mb-0">Tidak ada aktivitas terdeteksi. Terminal akan terkunci otomatis dalam 1 menit.</p>',
                                icon: 'warning',
                                confirmButtonText: '<i class="bi bi-arrow-clockwise me-1"></i> Lanjutkan Sesi',
                                customClass: { confirmButton: 'btn btn-warning rounded-pill px-4 fw-bold shadow-sm' },
                                buttonsStyling: false,
                                timer: CFG.IDLE_WARN_MS
                            }).then((r) => { if (r.isConfirmed) resetIdle(); });
                        }
                    } catch (e) {}
                }, Math.max(0, CFG.IDLE_MS - CFG.IDLE_WARN_MS));

                _idleTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                    if (!SESSION.authenticated) return;
                    lock({ reason: 'idle' });
                }, CFG.IDLE_MS);
            }

            function armIdleWatch() {
                if (!_idleArmed) {
                    IDLE_EVENTS.forEach(ev => document.addEventListener(ev, resetIdle, { passive: true }));
                    _idleArmed = true;
                }
                resetIdle();
            }

            function disarmIdleWatch() { _clearIdleTimers(); }

            // ── LOGOUT / KUNCI ULANG ─────────────────────────────────────────
            function logout(event) {
                if (event) event.preventDefault();
                if (typeof Swal === 'undefined') { lock({ reason: 'manual' }); return false; }

                Swal.fire({
                    title: 'Akhiri Sesi?',
                    html: '<p class="text-muted mb-0">Anda akan keluar dari terminal dan kembali ke halaman otorisasi.</p>',
                    icon: 'question',
                    showCancelButton: true,
                    confirmButtonText: '<i class="bi bi-power me-1"></i> Ya, Log Out',
                    cancelButtonText: 'Batal',
                    reverseButtons: true,
                    customClass: {
                        confirmButton: 'btn btn-danger rounded-pill px-4 shadow-sm fw-bold',
                        cancelButton : 'btn btn-light border rounded-pill px-4 fw-bold me-2'
                    },
                    buttonsStyling: false
                }).then((r) => { if (r.isConfirmed) lock({ reason: 'manual' }); });

                return false;
            }

            /** Teardown penuh → kembali ke gateway dalam kondisi bersih. */
            function lock(opt) {
                const reason = (opt && opt.reason) || 'manual';

                SESSION.user = null;
                SESSION.authenticated = false;

                // 🧹 Satu panggilan → APP_SESSION dikosongkan + 'onSessionCleared'
                //    disiarkan, sehingga Profile & modul lain MENGHAPUS data pemilik
                //    sesi sebelumnya (dulu data lama tetap terpampang setelah logout).
                JSSession.clear();

                try {
                    if (window.JSEngine && window.JSEngine.session && typeof window.JSEngine.session.clear === 'function') {
                        window.JSEngine.session.clear();
                    }
                } catch (e) {}

                disarmIdleWatch();
                applyRoleVisibility(null);

                // Tutup semua overlay yatim sebelum layer gateway dipasang.
                try {
                    if (typeof cleanupModalOrphans === 'function') cleanupModalOrphans();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.modal.show').forEach(el => {
                        const inst = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(el);
                        if (inst) inst.hide();
                    });
                    document.querySelectorAll('.dropdown-menu.show').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('show'));
                    if (window.JSShell) window.JSShell.closePalette();
                    if (typeof Swal !== 'undefined') Swal.close();
                    if (typeof closeScanner      === 'function') closeScanner();
                    if (typeof closePrimeCamera  === 'function') closePrimeCamera();
                } catch (e) {}

                // Reset field
                const akunInp  = $('loginNamaAkun');
                const sandiInp = $('loginKataSandi');
                if (sandiInp) { sandiInp.value = ''; sandiInp.type = 'password'; }
                const eyeIcon = $('loginEyeIcon');
                if (eyeIcon) eyeIcon.className = 'bi bi-eye-fill';
                const capsHint = $('loginCapsHint');
                if (capsHint) capsHint.classList.remove('show');
                clearError();
                _submitting = false;
                setLoading(false);

                const punyaMemori = restoreRemember();
                if (!punyaMemori && akunInp) akunInp.value = '';

                // 🔒 Pasang kunci layer & bersihkan seluruh state transisi
                document.body.classList.add('gateway-locked');
                document.body.classList.remove('app-ready', 'app-booting', 'backend-booting');
                if (typeof hideLoader === 'function') hideLoader();

                const gateway = $('login-gateway');
                if (gateway) {
                    if (_hideTimer) clearTimeout(_hideTimer);
                    gateway.style.display = 'flex';
                    void gateway.offsetWidth;                     // paksa reflow sebelum transisi
                    gateway.classList.remove('gateway-hidden');
                }

                setTimeout(() => {
                    const fokus = punyaMemori ? $('loginKataSandi') : $('loginNamaAkun');
                    if (fokus) fokus.focus();
                }, 450);

                if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
                    showToast(reason === 'idle'
                        ? 'Sesi terkunci otomatis karena tidak ada aktivitas.'
                        : 'Sesi berhasil diakhiri. Silakan login kembali.', reason === 'idle' ? 'warning' : 'info');
                }
            }

            // ── BINDING ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            function bind() {
                const akun  = $('loginNamaAkun');
                const sandi = $('loginKataSandi');

                if (!_bound) {
                    _bound = true;
                    const form   = $('loginForm');
                    const toggle = $('loginTogglePass');

                    // ✅ SATU jalur submit: event 'submit' menangani klik tombol
                    //    DAN tombol Enter sekaligus. Kombinasi lama (onsubmit
                    //    return false + onclick + listener keypress) membuat
                    //    Enter memicu executeLogin() DUA KALI.
                    if (form)   form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); login(); });
                    if (toggle) toggle.addEventListener('click', togglePass);

                    [akun, sandi].forEach(inp => {
                        if (!inp) return;
                        inp.addEventListener('input', clearError);
                        inp.addEventListener('keyup',   handleCaps);
                        inp.addEventListener('keydown', handleCaps);
                    });
                    if (sandi) sandi.addEventListener('blur', () => {
                        const h = $('loginCapsHint'); if (h) h.classList.remove('show');
                    });
                }

                const punyaMemori = restoreRemember();
                restoreLockout();

                setTimeout(() => {
                    const fokus = punyaMemori ? sandi : akun;
                    if (fokus) fokus.focus();
                }, 420);
            }

            return {
                bind, login, logout, togglePass,
                lock, isLocked,
                isAuthenticated: () => SESSION.authenticated === true,
                getUser: () => SESSION.user
            };
        })();

        // 🔗 Kompatibilitas nama global lama (dipakai markup & modul lain).
        window.AuthGateway    = AuthGateway;
        window.executeLogin   = function ()      { AuthGateway.login(); };
        window.executeLogout  = function (event) { return AuthGateway.logout(event); };
        window.toggleLoginPass= function ()      { AuthGateway.togglePass(); };

        // =========================================================================
        // 🔗 BAB C: JS CORE EVENT BUS (HARDENED)
        // =========================================================================
        const JSCoreEventBus = new EventTarget();

        window.tembakEventJS = function(namaEvent, payloadData = null) {
            console.log(`[JS PRIME EVENT] ${namaEvent}`, payloadData);
            JSCoreEventBus.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(namaEvent, { detail: payloadData }));
        };
        window.dengarEventJS = function(namaEvent, callback) {
            JSCoreEventBus.addEventListener(namaEvent, (e) => callback(e.detail));
        };

        // 🚀 Jembatan resmi: StockTab lapor boot selesai → buka gerbang backend.
        window.dengarEventJS('onStockTabBooted', function() {
            window.JSBackend.openGate('stock_tab-synced');
        });

        // 🔧 [FIX B1] Guard activeModule null → cegah polusi key "null".
        window.dengarEventJS('onStockMutated', function() {
            console.log('[JS PRIME] DB Mutated → kalibrasi flag sinkronisasi tertunda.');
            const active = APP_STATE.activeModule;
            Object.keys(APP_STATE.dirtyModules).forEach(k => {
                if (k !== active) APP_STATE.dirtyModules[k] = true;
            });
            // Modul aktif TIDAK dirty: forceSync jalur submit sudah menyegarkannya.
            if (active && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(APP_STATE.dirtyModules, active)) {
                APP_STATE.dirtyModules[active] = false;
            }
        });

        // =========================================================================
        // 🧭 LAZY-SYNC ENGINE (SINGLE-FIRE INIT + DIRTY SYNC + SUB-TAB ROUTE)
        // =========================================================================
        const MODULE_REGISTRY = {
            'stock_aktif'        : () => window.StockAktifModule,
            'stock_opname'       : () => window.StockOpnameModule,
            'stock_nonaktif'     : () => window.StockNonaktifModule,
            'jurnal_manager'     : () => window.JurnalModule,
            // ⛔ 'stock_control' DIHAPUS: containernya (#mod-stock_control) sudah
            //    tidak ada, jadi switchModule mustahil menjangkaunya. Entri yang
            //    tertinggal hanya akan memanggil window.StockControlModule yang
            //    selamanya undefined.
            'stock_tab'          : () => window.StockTabModule,
            'dashboard_stock'    : () => window.DashboardStockModule,
            'configuration_tab'  : () => window.ConfigurationTabModule,
            // ⛔ 'kode_stock' DIHAPUS dari registry: tidak punya container
            //    #mod-kode_stock, jadi switchModule mustahil menjangkaunya.
            //    KodeStockModule dirender sebagai sub-tab di configuration_tab.
            'quality_control_tab': () => window.QualityControlModule,
            'dashboard_transaction': () => window.DashboardTransactionModule,
            'goldboard_tab'      : () => window.GoldboardTabModule,
            // Container legacy (masih dirender oleh include) — didaftarkan agar
            // tidak menjadi layar yatim bila suatu saat dirutekan langsung.
            'goldboard_penjualan': () => window.GoldboardPenjualanModule,
            'goldboard_pembelian': () => window.GoldboardPembelianModule,
            'goldboard_perawatan': () => window.GoldboardPerawatanModule,
            'transaksi_penjualan': () => window.PenjualanModule,
            'transaksi_pembelian': () => window.PembelianModule,
            'transaksi_peduli'   : () => window.PeduliModule,
            'transaksi_perawatan': () => window.PerawatanModule,
            'transaksi_pemasukan': () => window.PemasukanModule,
            'transaksi_pengeluaran': () => window.PengeluaranModule,
            'transaksi_keuangan' : () => window.KeuanganModule,
            'social_media'       : () => window.SocialMediaModule,
            // 🔧 [FIX] Modul Profile secara historis mengekspor `window.PFMod`,
            //    sementara registry mencari `window.ProfileModule` → lazy-sync
            //    engine TIDAK PERNAH memanggil init()/forceSync() Profile.
            //    Resolver kini menerima kedua nama.
            'profile'            : () => window.ProfileModule || window.PFMod,
            'user_manager'       : () => window.UserManagerModule,
            'schedule_manager'   : () => window.ScheduleModule,
            'task_manager'       : () => window.TaskModule,
            'media_manager'      : () => window.MediaModule
        };
        const TABBED_MODULES = ['stock_tab', 'configuration_tab', 'quality_control_tab', 'goldboard_tab'];

        /**
         * 🧮 Derivasi peta dirty — sumber kebenaran TUNGGAL = MODULE_REGISTRY.
         * Hanya modul yang benar-benar punya container #mod-* yang didaftarkan,
         * sehingga tidak ada lagi key yatim maupun modul yang terlewat.
         */
        Object.keys(MODULE_REGISTRY).forEach((mod) => {
            if (document.getElementById(`mod-${mod}`)) APP_STATE.dirtyModules[mod] = true;
        });

        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           🔌 ADAPTER KONTRAK MODUL
           -------------------------------------------------------------------
           Akar bug "menu sidebar mati": Index memanggil `routeToTab()` /
           `switchSubTab()`, sementara ConfigurationTab hanya mengekspor
           `switchTab()`. Tidak ada satu pun nama yang beririsan → FASE 3
           berjalan tanpa efek, dan sub-menu Configuration di sidebar hanya
           bekerja SEKALI (lewat init) lalu diam selamanya.

           Solusinya bukan menambal satu modul, melainkan menjadikan Index
           toleran terhadap seluruh dialek nama yang dipakai modul-modul di
           proyek ini — sekaligus BERTERIAK di console bila sebuah modul
           bertab tidak menyediakan satu pun kontrak, supaya kasus serupa
           ketahuan saat pengembangan, bukan setelah dipakai kasir.
           ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        const TAB_ROUTE_METHODS = ['routeToTab', 'switchSubTab', 'switchTab', 'routeTo', 'gotoTab'];
        const SYNC_METHODS      = ['forceSync', 'syncData', 'reload', 'refresh'];

        /** Panggil metode pertama yang tersedia. @returns {string|null} nama yang dipakai */
        function _invokeFirst(M, names, args) {
            if (!M) return null;
            for (const n of names) {
                if (typeof M[n] === 'function') {
                    M[n].apply(M, args || []);
                    return n;
                }
            }
            return null;
        }

        function _syncModule(mod, tab) {
            const M = MODULE_REGISTRY[mod] ? MODULE_REGISTRY[mod]() : null;
            if (!M) return;
            // Modul bertab menerima tab aktif agar menyegarkan panel yang benar,
            // bukan panel default-nya.
            const args = TABBED_MODULES.includes(mod) && tab ? [tab] : [];
            const dipakai = _invokeFirst(M, SYNC_METHODS, args);
            if (!dipakai) console.warn(`[JS PRIME] Modul "${mod}" tak punya kontrak sinkronisasi (${SYNC_METHODS.join('/')}).`);
        }

        function _routeTab(mod, tab) {
            const M = MODULE_REGISTRY[mod] ? MODULE_REGISTRY[mod]() : null;
            if (!M || !tab) return;
            const dipakai = _invokeFirst(M, TAB_ROUTE_METHODS, [tab]);
            if (!dipakai) {
                console.error(
                    `[JS PRIME] ⛔ Modul bertab "${mod}" tidak mengekspor satu pun dari ` +
                    `${TAB_ROUTE_METHODS.join(' / ')} → klik sub-menu sidebar TIDAK akan berpindah tab.`);
            }
        }

        window.dengarEventJS('onModuleSwitched', function(payload) {
            const targetMod = payload.moduleName;
            const targetTab = payload.targetTab;
            const isSame    = payload.isSameModule === true;

            setTimeout(() => {
                const bertab = TABBED_MODULES.includes(targetMod);
                let baruInit = false;

                // ── FASE 1: SINGLE-FIRE INIT ────────────────────────────────
                if (!APP_STATE.initializedModules[targetMod]) {
                    console.log(`[JS PRIME] Bootstrapping UI: ${targetMod}`);
                    try {
                        const M = MODULE_REGISTRY[targetMod] ? MODULE_REGISTRY[targetMod]() : null;
                        if (M && typeof M.init === 'function') {
                            bertab ? M.init(targetTab) : M.init();
                            baruInit = true;
                        }
                        APP_STATE.initializedModules[targetMod] = true;
                    } catch (e) {
                        console.warn(`[JS PRIME] Init Fail [${targetMod}]:`, e.message);
                    }
                }

                // ── FASE 2: DIRTY SYNC ──────────────────────────────────────
                // [FIX B2] Klik modul yang sama & tidak dirty → skip sync (idempotent).
                if (APP_STATE.dirtyModules[targetMod] === true) {
                    console.log(`[JS PRIME] Lazy sync: ${targetMod}`);
                    _syncModule(targetMod, targetTab);
                    APP_STATE.dirtyModules[targetMod] = false;
                } else if (isSame) {
                    console.log(`[JS PRIME] Modul sama & bersih → skip sync (${targetMod}).`);
                }

                // ── FASE 3: SUB-TAB ROUTING ─────────────────────────────────
                // Dilewati bila init(tab) baru saja dijalankan pada pass ini —
                // init sudah membuka tab yang diminta, merutekan lagi hanya
                // menggandakan kerja (dan pada sebagian modul memicu fetch dobel).
                if (bertab && !baruInit) {
                    try { _routeTab(targetMod, targetTab); }
                    catch (e) { console.warn(`[JS PRIME] Sub-tab route error ${targetMod}:`, e); }
                }
            }, 250);
        });

        // =========================================================================
        // GLOBAL HELPER FUNCTIONS
        // =========================================================================
        /**
         * 🖨️ Router ekspor PDF — resolusi lewat MODULE_REGISTRY.
         * Rantai if/else lama harus disunting manual tiap ada modul baru dan
         * diam-diam gagal bila nama global modul berubah.
         */
        window.executeGlobalPDF = function() {
            const active = APP_STATE.activeModule;
            const M = (active && MODULE_REGISTRY[active]) ? MODULE_REGISTRY[active]() : null;
            if (M && typeof M.generatePDFServer === 'function') {
                try { M.generatePDFServer(); }
                catch (e) {
                    console.error('[PDF] Gagal:', e);
                    showToast('Ekspor PDF gagal dijalankan modul aktif.', 'danger');
                }
                return;
            }
            showToast('Modul aktif tidak mendukung ekspor PDF saat ini.', 'warning');
        };

        function cleanupModalOrphans() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.modal-backdrop').forEach(el => el.remove());
            document.body.classList.remove('modal-open');
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
            document.body.style.paddingRight = '';
            // 🔧 [FIX B11] tutup Swal yatim agar tidak menahan overlay antar-modul
            try { if (typeof Swal !== 'undefined' && Swal.isVisible()) Swal.close(); } catch(e) {}
        }


        let _loaderHideTimer = null;
        let _loaderShownAt   = 0;

        /** Ganti keterangan tirai dengan silang-pudar, bukan lompatan teks. */
        function setLoaderCaption(text) {
            const cap = document.getElementById('loaderCaption');
            if (!cap || !text || cap.textContent === text) return;
            cap.classList.add('is-swapping');
            setTimeout(() => { cap.textContent = text; cap.classList.remove('is-swapping'); }, 200);
        }
        window.setLoaderCaption = setLoaderCaption;

        /**
         * @param {string}  text
         * @param {boolean} instant  true = tampil tanpa fade-in. Dipakai ketika
         *   tirai harus SUDAH utuh di balik gateway sebelum gateway memudar;
         *   fade-in 0,55 dtk di sini justru membuat dashboard sempat terintip.
         */
        function showLoader(text, instant) {
            const loader = document.getElementById('global-loader');
            if (!loader) return;
            if (_loaderHideTimer) { clearTimeout(_loaderHideTimer); _loaderHideTimer = null; }

            const cap = document.getElementById('loaderCaption');
            if (cap && text) {
                if (instant) { cap.classList.remove('is-swapping'); cap.textContent = text; }
                else setLoaderCaption(text);
            }

            loader.classList.toggle('loader-instant', !!instant);
            loader.style.display = 'flex';
            void loader.offsetWidth;                       // paksa reflow sebelum transisi
            loader.classList.remove('loader-hidden');
            _loaderShownAt = Date.now();

            if (instant) {
                // Lepas mode instan setelah satu bingkai agar fade-out tetap halus.
                requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => loader.classList.remove('loader-instant')));
            }
        }

        /**
         * @param {number} minMs  durasi tampil minimum. Tanpa ini tirai bisa
         *   muncul-hilang dalam < 100 ms pada koneksi cepat → terlihat seperti
         *   kedipan/glitch, bukan transisi.
         */
        function hideLoader(minMs) {
            const loader = document.getElementById('global-loader');
            if (!loader) return;

            const sisa = Math.max(0, (Number(minMs) || 0) - (Date.now() - _loaderShownAt));
            if (_loaderHideTimer) { clearTimeout(_loaderHideTimer); _loaderHideTimer = null; }

            const tutup = () => {
                loader.classList.remove('loader-instant');
                loader.classList.add('loader-hidden');
                _loaderHideTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                    if (loader.classList.contains('loader-hidden')) loader.style.display = 'none';
                }, 650);
            };
            if (sisa > 0) _loaderHideTimer = setTimeout(tutup, sisa);
            else tutup();
        }

        let _toastSeq = 0;

        /**
         * 🔔 showToast — dipakai 8+ modul, jadi WAJIB tahan input apa pun.
         * [FIX] Sebelumnya `msg.includes(...)` meledak bila pemanggil mengirim
         * Error/objek/null, dan pesan disuntik mentah lewat innerHTML.
         * Kini: koersi aman ke string + penulisan via textContent.
         */
        function showToast(msg, type = 'success') {
            const container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
            if (!container) return;

            let text = (msg instanceof Error) ? msg.message
                     : (msg === null || msg === undefined) ? ''
                     : (typeof msg === 'object') ? (msg.message || JSON.stringify(msg))
                     : String(msg);
            if (!text.trim()) return;

            const colors = { success: 'border-success', warning: 'border-warning', danger: 'border-danger', info: 'border-info' };
            const icons  = { success: 'bi-check-circle-fill text-success', warning: 'bi-exclamation-triangle-fill text-warning', danger: 'bi-x-circle-fill text-danger', info: 'bi-info-circle-fill text-info' };

            if (text.includes('Timeout') || text.includes('Sistem sibuk')) {
                text = 'Sistem sedang padat oleh aktivitas User lain. Tunggu 5 detik lalu coba lagi.';
                type = 'warning';
            }

            const id = 'toast-' + (Date.now()) + '-' + (++_toastSeq);   // anti-tabrakan id
            const el = document.createElement('div');
            el.id = id;
            el.className = `toast align-items-center bg-white text-dark border-start border-4 ${colors[type] || colors.info} shadow-lg show`;
            el.setAttribute('role', 'alert');
            el.style.borderRadius = '8px';
            el.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="d-flex">
                        <div class="toast-body fw-bold py-3 d-flex align-items-center">
                            <i class="bi ${icons[type] || icons.info} fs-4 me-3"></i>
                            <span class="toast-msg"></span>
                        </div>
                        <button type="button" class="btn-close me-3 m-auto shadow-none" aria-label="Tutup"></button>
                    </div>`;
            el.querySelector('.toast-msg').textContent = text;

            const dismiss = () => {
                if (!el.isConnected) return;
                el.style.transition = 'opacity 0.4s ease';
                el.style.opacity = '0';
                setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 400);
            };
            el.querySelector('.btn-close').addEventListener('click', dismiss);

            container.appendChild(el);
            setTimeout(dismiss, 3500);
        }

                function viewImage(urlOrPath) {
            if (!urlOrPath || urlOrPath === '-' || String(urlOrPath).trim() === '') {
                showToast('Aset tidak memiliki visualisasi HD.', 'warning');
                return;
            }
            const s = String(urlOrPath).trim();
 
            // 1) Data URI matang → tampilkan langsung
            if (s.startsWith('data:image')) { openImageModal(s); return; }
 
            // 2) Deteksi wilayah Drive: URL drive/googleusercontent, ID mentah,
            //    atau path AppSheet (non-http) → WAJIB via server
            const isDriveRealm = /drive\.google\.com|googleusercontent\.com/.test(s)
                              || /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{25,45}$/.test(s)
                              || !s.startsWith('http');
 
            // 3) URL http eksternal murni (non-Google) → aman langsung
            if (!isDriveRealm) { openImageModal(s); return; }
 
            showToast('Menarik resolusi HD dari Server Drive...', 'info');
            document.body.style.cursor = 'wait';
 
            const onDone = (res) => {
                document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
                if (res && res.status && res.dataUri) openImageModal(res.dataUri);
                else showToast((res && res.message) || 'File asli telah dihapus dari Drive.', 'danger');
            };
            const onFail = () => {
                document.body.style.cursor = 'default';
                showToast('Koneksi terputus saat mengambil gambar HD.', 'danger');
            };
 
            if (window.JSBackend && typeof window.JSBackend.run === 'function') {
                window.JSBackend.run('apiGetImageHDBase64', [s], { owner: 'core_viewer', onSuccess: onDone, onFailure: onFail });
            } else {
                google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(onDone).withFailureHandler(onFail).apiGetImageHDBase64(s);
            }
        }


        function openImageModal(url) {
            const imgEl   = document.getElementById('fullSizeImage');
            const modalEl = document.getElementById('imageViewerModal');
            if (!imgEl || !modalEl) return;                  // guard: modal belum ter-render
            imgEl.src = url;
            const imgModal = bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(modalEl);
            imgModal.show();

            modalEl.addEventListener('hidden.bs.modal', function () {
                imgEl.src = '';
            }, { once: true });
        }

        /**
         * 🔳 renderQRCode — penulis QR SISI KLIEN.
         * chart.googleapis.com (Google Image Charts) dimatikan 19 Maret 2019,
         * sehingga seluruh <img src="chart.googleapis.com/...qr..."> di sistem ini
         * merender gambar rusak. Kini QR digambar lokal via qrcodejs (canvas),
         * tanpa permintaan jaringan, tanpa rate limit, dan tetap tampil offline.
         * @returns {boolean} true bila berhasil digambar.
         */
        window.renderQRCode = function (container, text, size) {
            const host = (typeof container === 'string') ? document.getElementById(container) : container;
            const isi  = String(text || '').trim();
            if (!host || !isi) return false;

            host.innerHTML = '';
            const px = Number(size) || 220;

            if (typeof QRCode === 'undefined') {
                host.innerHTML = '<div class="text-muted small fw-bold p-3">Pustaka QR belum termuat.</div>';
                console.warn('[QR] qrcodejs tidak tersedia.');
                return false;
            }
            try {
                new QRCode(host, {
                    text: isi, width: px, height: px,
                    colorDark: '#0f172a', colorLight: '#ffffff',
                    correctLevel: QRCode.CorrectLevel.M
                });
                const el = host.querySelector('canvas, img');
                if (el) { el.style.width = px + 'px'; el.style.height = px + 'px'; el.style.display = 'block'; }
                return true;
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('[QR] Gagal merender:', e);
                host.innerHTML = '<div class="text-muted small fw-bold p-3">QR gagal dirender.</div>';
                return false;
            }
        };

        function showQRCode(barcodeText) {
            if (!barcodeText || barcodeText === '-') {
                showToast('Sistem tidak mendeteksi ID Barcode.', 'warning');
                return;
            }
            const clean = String(barcodeText).toUpperCase().trim();

            Swal.fire({
                title: '<i class="bi bi-qr-code me-2 text-dark"></i>Digital Node Identifier',
                html: `
                    <div class="text-center p-3">
                        <div class="bg-white p-3 rounded-4 shadow-sm border d-inline-block mb-3 hover-lift"
                             id="swalQrHost" style="min-width:246px;min-height:246px;"></div>
                        <h4 class="fw-900 text-dark mb-0" style="letter-spacing:2px;"></h4>
                        <p class="text-muted small mt-1">Gunakan pemindai optik untuk verifikasi node.</p>
                    </div>`,
                confirmButtonText: '<i class="bi bi-check-circle me-1"></i> Selesai',
                customClass: { confirmButton: 'btn btn-dark rounded-pill px-4 shadow-sm fw-bold' },
                buttonsStyling: false,
                didOpen: () => {
                    // ⛔ Nilai barcode ditulis via textContent, bukan template HTML,
                    //    agar isi kode tak pernah ditafsirkan sebagai markup.
                    const cap = document.querySelector('.swal2-html-container h4');
                    if (cap) cap.textContent = clean;
                    window.renderQRCode('swalQrHost', clean, 220);
                }
            });
        }

        /**
         * 🔧 [FIX B8] `apiUpdateDeskripsi` TIDAK ADA di Code.gs.
         * Dialihkan ke endpoint resmi: apiUpdateInlineCell(rowIndex, columnName, newValue, barcode, actorId).
         * Barcode WAJIB — tanpa itu Code.gs menolak (integrity guard).
         */
        function editDeskripsiBarang(rowIndex, currentDeskripsi, barcode) {
            if (!barcode || barcode === '-') {
                showToast('Barcode aset tidak terdeteksi. Koreksi deskripsi dibatalkan.', 'warning');
                return;
            }
            Swal.fire({
                title: 'Koreksi Deskripsi',
                input: 'textarea',
                inputLabel: 'Masukkan detail deskripsi operasional baru:',
                inputValue: currentDeskripsi || '',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonText: '<i class="bi bi-save me-1"></i> Terapkan',
                cancelButtonText: 'Batal',
                inputValidator: (v) => (!v ? 'Parameter deskripsi tidak valid (Kosong).' : undefined),
                customClass: { confirmButton: 'btn btn-primary shadow-sm', cancelButton: 'btn btn-light border' }
            }).then((r) => {
                if (!r.isConfirmed) return;
                const val   = r.value;
                const actor = (window.APP_SESSION && window.APP_SESSION.userId) || 'SISTEM';
                showToast('Mengkalibrasi database server...', 'info');

                window.JSBackend.run('apiUpdateInlineCell',
                    [rowIndex, 'DESKRIPSI BARANG', val, barcode, actor], {
                    owner: 'core_editor',
                    onSuccess: (res) => {
                        if (res && (res.status === true || res.status === 'success')) {
                            showToast('Deskripsi tersinkronisasi!', 'success');
                            const el = document.getElementById('detail_deskripsi');
                            if (el) el.innerText = val;
                            window.tembakEventJS('onStockMutated');
                        } else {
                            showToast((res && res.message) || 'Node Error: Gagal menyimpan data', 'danger');
                        }
                    },
                    onFailure: () => showToast('Koneksi sistem terputus.', 'danger')
                });
            });
        }

        // =========================================================================
        // HARDWARE SCANNER INTEGRATION
        // =========================================================================
        async function globalForceCameraInit() {
            try {
                const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: true, audio: false });
                if (stream) stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
            } catch (err) {
                console.warn("[JS PRIME] Protokol Lensa tertahan:", err.message);
            }
        }

        function openScanner() {
            const scannerModalEl = document.getElementById('scannerModal');
            const statusEl = document.getElementById('scannerStatus');
            if (!scannerModalEl || !statusEl) return;
            // getOrCreateInstance: `new bootstrap.Modal()` berulang menumpuk backdrop.
            const modal = bootstrap.Modal.getOrCreateInstance(scannerModalEl);

            modal.show();
            statusEl.innerHTML = '<i class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm text-primary me-2"></i>Inisialisasi Perangkat Optik...';

            if (html5QrCode) { try { html5QrCode.clear(); } catch(e) {} }

            html5QrCode = new Html5Qrcode("reader");
            const config = { fps: 15, qrbox: { width: 250, height: 250 }, aspectRatio: 1.0 };

            Html5Qrcode.getCameras().then(devices => {
                if (devices && devices.length > 0) {
                    let cameraId = devices[0].id;
                    for (let i = 0; i < devices.length; i++) {
                        let label = devices[i].label.toLowerCase();
                        if (label.includes("back") || label.includes("belakang") || label.includes("environment")) {
                            cameraId = devices[i].id; break;
                        }
                    }

                    html5QrCode.start(
                        cameraId, config,
                        (decodedText, decodedResult) => {
                            showToast('Protokol Dikenali: ' + decodedText, 'success');
                            closeScanner();

                            // ---- ROUTING HASIL SCAN KE MODUL ----
                            const searchInput = document.getElementById('stockUniversalSearch') || document.getElementById('filterKeyword') || document.getElementById('filterOpnameSearch');
                            if (APP_STATE.activeModule === 'penjualan' && window.PenjualanModule) {
                                window.PenjualanModule.handleBarcode(decodedText);
                            }
                            else if (searchInput) {
                                searchInput.value = decodedText;
                                if (APP_STATE.activeModule === 'stock_aktif' && window.StockAktifModule) window.StockAktifModule.applyFilters();
                                else if (APP_STATE.activeModule === 'stock_opname' && window.StockOpnameModule) window.StockOpnameModule.applyFilters();
                                else if (APP_STATE.activeModule === 'stock_nonaktif' && window.StockNonaktifModule) window.StockNonaktifModule.applyFilters();
                                else if (APP_STATE.activeModule === 'stock_tab' && window.StockTabModule) window.StockTabModule.applyFilters();
                            }
                        },
                        (errorMessage) => {}
                    ).then(() => {
                        statusEl.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-upc-scan text-success me-2"></i>Lensa Siap. Arahkan ke target Barcode/QR.';
                    }).catch((err) => {
                        statusEl.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-x-circle text-danger me-2"></i>Koneksi Stream Gagal.';
                        showToast('Stream Optik terblokir sistem.', 'danger');
                    });

                } else {
                    statusEl.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle text-warning me-2"></i>Hardware kamera tidak terlacak.';
                }
            }).catch(err => {
                statusEl.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-x-circle text-danger me-2"></i>Izin Perangkat Ditolak.';
                showToast('Sistem browser memblokir akses lensa.', 'danger');
            });
        }

        function closeScanner() {
            const modalEl = document.getElementById('scannerModal');
            const modal = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(modalEl);
            if (modal) modal.hide();

            if (html5QrCode) {
                try {
